Multiply across loci.</i></p>	3 &nbsp; = 3 (<i>i.e.</i> 1 gene with three forms)	Incorrect	2<sup>2</sup> &nbsp; = 4 (<i>i.e.</i> 2 genes with two forms)	Correct	2<sup>3</sup> &nbsp; = 8 (<i>i.e.</i> 3 genes with two forms)	Incorrect	2 &times; 3<sup>2</sup> &nbsp; = 18 (<i>i.e.</i> 1 gene with two forms and 2 genes with three forms)	Incorrect	2<sup>2</sup> &times; 3<sup>2</sup> &nbsp; = 36 (<i>i.e.</i> 2 genes with two forms and 2 genes with three forms)	Incorrect	3<sup>4</sup> &nbsp; = 81 (<i>i.e.</i> 4 genes with three forms)	Incorrect

Multiply across loci.</i></p>	3 &nbsp; = 3 (<i>i.e.</i> 1 gene with three forms)	Incorrect	2<sup>2</sup> &nbsp; = 4 (<i>i.e.</i> 2 genes with two forms)	Correct	2 &times; 3 &nbsp; = 6 (<i>i.e.</i> 1 gene with two forms and 1 gene with three forms)	Incorrect	3<sup>2</sup> &nbsp; = 9 (<i>i.e.</i> 2 genes with three forms)	Incorrect	2<sup>4</sup> &nbsp; = 16 (<i>i.e.</i> 4 genes with two forms)	Incorrect	2 &times; 3<sup>2</sup> &nbsp; = 18 (<i>i.e.</i> 1 gene with two forms and 2 genes with three forms)	Incorrect
